Use before finalizing any commit or opening a PR in this repo, especially from a worktree or agent session where it's easy to stage only the files a task explicitly touched — local test runs silently regenerate these fixtures and it's easy to leave them out of the changeset. Applies to Dart/Flutter with Mockito, but pattern is universal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-abundance-filtering))
- **mock-community-benchmark-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a new strain- or genome-comparison method needs quantitative accuracy validation on community data with known ground-truth composition, e.g. reproducing the inStrain vs dRep/MIDAS/StrainPhlAn benchmark on ZymoBIOMICS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-accuracy-evaluation))
- **mock-community-benchmark-analysis**: Use when paired-end 2 × 250 bp 16S mock community datasets are available and you need to rank OTU-retrieval pipelines by read preservation, OTU-count accuracy, or runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-analysis--8b11a39644af1e63))
- **mock-community-benchmark-analysis**: Use when a community of known composition (mock or synthetic gut) with sequenced shotgun reads is available and you must quantify which SSU extraction, reconstruction, or classification tool recovers the expected variants, taxa and runtimes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-analysis--d1276ffdc5622484))
- **mock-community-benchmark-data-analysis**: Use when deposited LMAS metric tables (global and per-reference) from mock metagenomic communities are available and the goal is to test whether assembler branding (genomic vs metagenomic) predicts performance on complex samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-data-analysis))
- **mock-community-benchmark-datasets**: Reference for validating or benchmarking a 16S rRNA gene sequence variant caller on data with known composition, or for reproducing runtime, memory, and variant-count comparisons between HashSeq and DADA2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-datasets))
- **mock-community-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when validating a species-resolved ARG profiling pipeline on real long-read mock communities with known supplier reference genomes, where estimated ARG abundances must be compared to expected profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-evaluation))
- **mock-community-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when a metabarcoding pipeline needs quantitative performance validation on a mock community with known taxonomic composition, e.g., comparing ASV inference (Swarm v2) vs OTU clustering (VSEARCH) precision/recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-evaluation--1340bd796d8723ca))
- **mock-community-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing taxonomic profilers or reference libraries on 16S mock community sequencing data, to quantify genus-/species-level abundance error or alpha-diversity deviation against known ground-truth composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-evaluation--f16da8d3b810a7b4))
- **mock-community-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when comparing taxonomic classification tools on simulated mock communities with known ground-truth abundance profiles, to quantify genus/species identification, abundance estimation accuracy, and read classification rate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-evaluation))
- **mock-community-benchmark-metrics-computation**: Use when a newly generated strain-separated metagenome assembly must be quantitatively benchmarked per recovered strain against known reference genomes from a mock community (Mock3, Mock9). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-metrics-computation--69f3ff4314498c39))
- **mock-community-benchmark-metrics-computation**: Use when you have simulated/metagenomic mock community samples with known taxonomic composition and need to quantify a profiler's precision, sensitivity or F1 per taxonomic rank and abundance threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-metrics-computation--5f5273da6c474d54))
- **mock-community-benchmark-reproduction**: Use when benchmarking taxonomic classifiers on mock community Illumina reads and you need to reproduce genus/phylum sensitivity and precision comparisons between protein-level (Kaiju) and k-mer-based (Kraken) classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmark-reproduction))
- **mock-community-benchmarking**: Use when you need to quantify how faithfully an rRNA amplicon classification tool recovers known taxon abundances, using an artificial mock community dataset with a known expected taxonomic composition as ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-benchmarking))
- **mock-community-composition-comparison**: Use when a 16S rRNA amplicon profile has been CopyRighter-corrected and a known expected mock community composition (equal 16S rRNA gene copies per species) is available to quantify accuracy by Hellinger/euclidean distance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-composition-comparison))
- **mock-community-counts-matrix-generation**: Use when benchmarking 16S amplicon taxonomic profilers on synthetic mock communities and you need comparable per-sample taxon abundance counts matrices per profiler–reference-library pairing as the substrate for genus- and species-level accuracy evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-counts-matrix-generation))
- **mock-community-error-rate-quantification**: Use when you have raw paired-end mock community reads and need to compare how representative-sequence inference methods (denoising vs OTU clustering) and filtering stringency affect erroneous-sequence rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-error-rate-quantification))
- **mock-community-ground-truth-comparison**: Use when classifying reads or reconstructed SSU sequences from a mock/synthetic community with a known expected taxonomy, and you need per-rank precision/recall/F-measure to compare classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-ground-truth-comparison))
- **mock-community-metagenome-analysis**: Use when Illumina paired-end plus 4 kbp/8 kbp mate-pair reads from a known-composition mock metagenome (e.g. DDBJ PRJDB4377) must be assembled with several assemblers and compared by NGA50 contiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-metagenome-analysis))
- **mock-community-otu-reproduction**: Use when reprocessing previously published nanopore amplicon mock-community reads (e.g., INC-Seq consensus reads from older R7 chemistry) to reproduce de novo OTU-level consensus sequences and validate accuracy against published baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-otu-reproduction))
- **mock-community-profiler-performance-statistics**: Use when per-sample benchmark metrics (sensitivity, specificity, log NRMSE, alpha diversity deviations) exist for multiple profiler-library pairings on the same mock community samples and you must determine which pairings significantly outperform others. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-profiler-performance-statistics))
- **mock-community-quality-assessment**: Use when comparing de novo short-read assembler performance on a known mock community standard with reference genomes available, to judge which assemblers produce accurate, contiguous assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-quality-assessment))
- **mock-community-quantitative-recovery-analysis**: Use when fastq data from mock (bulk) communities with known taxon composition and known input DNA amounts exist, and you must compare platform-specific recovery bias, e.g. Nanopore MinION vs Illumina MiSeq. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-quantitative-recovery-analysis))
- **mock-community-read-simulation**: Use when validating a taxonomic or functional profiling database against cross-kingdom false-positive mapping, by simulating mock-community FASTQ reads with ART and measuring mapping rates or specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-read-simulation))
- **mock-community-reference-based-evaluation**: Use when a known-composition mock metagenome (e.g. GIS20 bacteria with Illumina paired-end plus ONT or PacBio long reads) is being assembled and contiguity/accuracy must be compared across assemblers against reference genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-reference-based-evaluation))
- **mock-community-richness-quantification**: Use when finished DADA2 ASV tables from depth-subsampled mock community sequencing runs (e.g. ERR777696 downsampled from 100 to 1,600,000 read pairs; PRJNA626434 from 100 to 40,000 reads) are available and the goal is to evaluate how observed richness scales with sequencing depth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-richness-quantification))
- **mock-community-scaffold-completeness-evaluation**: Use when a strain-separated long-read metagenome assembly (e.g., assembly.scaffolds.fa from Strainberry on a mock community) must be evaluated for completeness and base-level accuracy against known reference strain genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-scaffold-completeness-evaluation))
- **mock-community-sensitivity-calculation**: Use when benchmarking taxonomic profilers (DADA2, QIIME 2, Mothur, PathoScope 2, Kraken 2) or reference libraries (SILVA 138, Greengenes 13_8, Kraken Standard, RefSeq) on 16S mock community data with known composition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-sensitivity-calculation))
- **mock-community-sequence-benchmarking**: Use when raw nanopore 16S amplicon reads from a mock community of known composition are available and you need to quantify sequence accuracy, aligned proportion, or OTU-count behavior of a de novo consensus workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-sequence-benchmarking))
- **mock-community-simulation-benchmarking**: Use when validating that simulated PacBio HiFi amplicon reads reproduce per-genome error profiles observed in real mock-community sequencing runs, e.g. before benchmarking ASV clustering or taxonomic assignment pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-simulation-benchmarking))
- **mock-community-species-benchmarking**: Use when a target-detection tool must be quantitatively validated on a defined mock community, e.g. per-species read classification accuracy/F1 for 16S MinION reads under stratified 5-fold cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-species-benchmarking))
- **mock-community-species-recovery-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a COI metabarcoding pipeline against a mock community of known composition (e.g. Bista et al.) to measure species recovery and genus-level F1-scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-species-recovery-evaluation))
- **mock-community-strain-count-comparison**: Use when benchmarking OTU-count fidelity of 16S pipelines on mock communities with known strain composition, to judge which pipeline's OTU count deviates least from truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/mock-community-strain-count-comparison))
- **mock-community-taxa-comparison**: Use when amplicon ASV tables from one or more 16S hypervariable regions are available alongside a ZymoBIOMICS mock community control, and the goal is to quantify which region(s) classify taxa with the sensitivity/specificity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-taxa-comparison))
- **mock-community-taxonomic-classification**: Use when you need to benchmark that a DADA2-based amplicon pipeline correctly recovers known community richness (e.g. exactly 59 prokaryotic ASVs) and genus composition from a mock community fastq dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/mock-community-taxonomic-classification))
- **mock-community-validation-analysis**: Use when you have predicted gene-family (KO) abundance tables from 16S data plus shotgun-derived KO annotations for the same or mock communities, and need to quantify prediction accuracy.
